\Phi({\tau})=\Phi^{0}\log({\tau})+\Phi^{1}+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\Phi({\tau})=\frac{\Phi^{0}}{\tau}+O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big)\text{ in }C^{\infty}(S^{n}).

Proof.

It suffices to prove that for any
K>0
there exists a unique solution of (
3.1
) such that the above expansions hold in
H^{K}(S^{n}).
We introduce the quantity
\widetilde{\Phi}=\Phi-\Phi^{0}\log({\tau})-\Phi^{1},
which satisfies the equation:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}\big)+\frac{1}{{\tau}}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}-4\Delta\widetilde{\Phi}=\psi\nabla\widetilde{\Phi}+F_{1}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})\cdot\log(\tau)+F_{2}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1}).

(3.2)

where
F_{1}
and
F_{2}
are bounded functions of
\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1},
and their angular derivatives. To obtain this equation we use the fact that
\Phi^{0}
and
\Phi^{1}
are Lie transported in time, so by (
2.35
) we have
\nabla_{\tau}\Phi^{0}=\tau\chi\cdot\Phi_{0}
and
\nabla_{\tau}\Phi^{1}=\tau\chi\cdot\Phi_{1}.

For any
k\geq 0
we obtain the commuted equation:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}\big)+\frac{1}{{\tau}}\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}-4\Delta\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}=\psi\nabla\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}+F_{1}^{k}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})\cdot\log(\tau)+F_{2}^{k}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})+F_{3}^{k}(\widetilde{\Phi}),

where we denote the error terms:

F_{1}^{k}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})=\nabla^{k}\big(F_{1}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})\big),\ F_{2}^{k}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})=\nabla^{k}\big(F_{2}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})\big),\ F_{3}^{k}(\widetilde{\Phi})=[\nabla_{\tau},\nabla^{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}-4[\Delta,\nabla^{k}]\widetilde{\Phi}-[\psi\nabla,\nabla^{k}]\widetilde{\Phi}.

We remark that using the smoothness of
\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1},
and the background metric
\not{g}
, we obtain that:

F_{1}^{k}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})\cdot\log(\tau)+F_{2}^{k}(\Phi^{0},\Phi^{1})=O_{k}\big(1+|\log\tau|\big).

We define
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}
to be the solution to (
3.2
) on
[\epsilon,1]\times S^{n}
with initial data
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}|_{\tau=\epsilon}=\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}|_{\tau=\epsilon}=0.
Contracting the commuted equation for
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}
with
\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}
, we obtain the standard energy estimate:

\big\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim_{k}\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|[\nabla^{k+1},\nabla_{\tau}]\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}|\log(\tau^{\prime})|\cdot\big\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|F_{1}^{k}(\Phi_{0},\Phi_{1})\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|F_{2}^{k}(\Phi_{0},\Phi_{1})\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|F_{3}^{k}(\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon})\big\|_{L^{2}}

We notice that in the above estimate we dropped the bulk term with a favorable sign. Also, it is essential that
\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}
vanishes to sufficiently high order at
\tau=0
in order to not have any initial data contribution. Moreover, due to Lemma
3.1
, we also have the terms

\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\|\nabla\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
and
\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
on the RHS, but these can be bounded using Gronwall.

Using the higher order version of the commutation formula (
2.12
), the smoothness of the background metric
\not{g}
, and the Gronwall inequality, we obtain the estimate:

\big\|\nabla^{k}\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla\nabla^{k}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim_{k}\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k+1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}\cdot\big(1+|\log\tau^{\prime}|\big)d\tau^{\prime}.

where the implicit constant depends on
k\geq 0.
On the other hand, using Lemma
3.1
, Gronwall, and the commutation formulas, we also have:

\big\|\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{H^{k}}\lesssim_{k}\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use our previous two estimates and Gronwall to obtain that for any
\tau\in[\epsilon,1]
:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{H^{k}}+\big\|\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|^{2}_{H^{k+1}}\lesssim_{k}\int_{\epsilon}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}\cdot\big(1+|\log\tau^{\prime}|\big)d\tau^{\prime}.

By taking the supremum on
[\epsilon,\tau]
in the above inequality for each
\tau\in[\epsilon,1]
, we obtain that:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}=O_{k}\big(\tau(1+|\log\tau|)\big).

We use this and a similar argument for
\big\|\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}
to obtain the bound:

\big\|\widetilde{\Phi}_{\epsilon}\big\|_{H^{k}}=O_{k}\big(\tau^{2}(1+|\log\tau|)^{2}\big).

Using the Banach-Alaoglu theorem and compactness, we obtain that for
K\ll k
there exists
\widetilde{\Phi}
a solution of (
3.2
) on
\mathcal{M}
such that:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\widetilde{\Phi}\big\|_{H^{K}}=O_{K}\big(\tau(1+|\log\tau|)^{2}\big),\ \big\|\widetilde{\Phi}\big\|_{H^{K}}=O_{K}\big(\tau^{2}(1+|\log\tau|)^{2}\big).

As a result, we obtain that
\Phi=\widetilde{\Phi}+\Phi^{0}\log({\tau})+\Phi^{1}
is a solution of (
3.1
) and satisfies the desired expansions. Finally, we remark that uniqueness follows by using our standard energy estimate on
[0,\tau]
for the difference of two solutions with the same asymptotic expansion.
∎

3.2
Lower order estimates

The goal of this section is to establish estimates that are lower order in terms of the number of angular derivatives. We point out that the estimates of this section are not sharp, but it is essential that we use only the quantities that appear on the right hand side of the estimates in Theorem
1.1
.

The lower order estimates are carried out in two parts. First, we prove estimates for an integer number of angular derivatives
m<M,
establishing (
1.23
), (
1.24
), and the bounds for the commutator term
\mathcal{C}.
We then also prove estimates for
M-\frac{1}{2}
derivatives of the singular component
\big(\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}.

3.2.1
Standard estimates

We first prove the lower order estimates for the singular component using the strategy outlined in Section
1.1.3
. We further decompose for every
m<M
:

\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}=\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}+\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2},

where using Proposition
3.1
we have that
\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1},\ \big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2}
are the solutions of (
1.21
) such that:

\displaystyle\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}({\tau})=2\nabla^{m}\mathcal{O}\log({\tau})+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}({\tau})=\frac{2\nabla^{m}\mathcal{O}}{\tau}+O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),

\displaystyle\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2}({\tau})=2(\log\nabla)\nabla^{m}\mathcal{O}+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2}({\tau})=O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big).

For convenience of notation, we often write
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}
and
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}
instead of
\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}
and
\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2}
. Using this decomposition, we prove the following lower order estimates on the singular component:

Proposition 3.2
.

Set
\eta=1/10
. The singular component satisfies the following estimates for any
m<M
:

\displaystyle\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\bigg\|\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}\text{ for }\tau\in\bigg(0,\frac{1}{2}\bigg],

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}\text{ for }\tau\in\bigg[\frac{1}{2},1\bigg],

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1+\eta}}\text{ for }\tau\in(0,1].

In particular, the singular component satisfies the estimate (
1.24
) for any
m<M
:

\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big(1+|\log\tau|^{2}\big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1+\eta}}.

Proof.

We start with the estimate for
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}.
Using the standard
\nabla_{\tau}
multiplier in (
1.21
), we get for
\tau\in(0,1]
:

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\big|_{\tau=0}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{\tau}]\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We notice that due to Lemma
3.1
, we also have the terms

\int_{0}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
and
\int_{0}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
on the RHS, but these can be bounded using Gronwall. By the commutation formula (
2.12
), Lemma
2.7
, and Gronwall, we get:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1+\eta}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

On the other hand, we also have the estimate:

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+\eta}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+\eta}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

Combining the last two bounds, we obtain the desired estimate for
\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2}.

We notice that
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}
satisfies the equation:

\nabla_{\tau}\bigg(\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg)+\frac{1}{\tau}\bigg(1+\frac{2}{\log\tau}\bigg)\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}-4\Delta\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}=\psi\nabla\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}.

We use
\nabla_{\tau}
as a multiplier to obtain the estimate for any
\tau\in(0,1/2]
:

\displaystyle\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\bigg\|\nabla\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigg\|\nabla\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg|_{\tau=0}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{\textbf{1}_{[1/10,1/2]}}{\tau^{\prime}|\log\tau^{\prime}|}\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}\bigg\|\nabla\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg\|\nabla\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}\bigg\|[\nabla,\nabla_{\tau}]\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}

.

We point out that the error term
\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}|\log\tau^{\prime}|}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}/\log\tau\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\cdot\textbf{1}_{[1/10,1/2]}
appears on the right hand side because for
\tau\in[0,1/10]
we have
1+2/\log\tau\gtrsim 1,
so the bulk term has a favorable sign. The error terms are estimated as usual, and we use Gronwall to obtain:

\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\bigg\|\nabla\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg\|\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We also have the estimate:

\bigg\|\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg\|\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg\|\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg\|\nabla_{\tau}\frac{\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}}{\log\tau}\bigg\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Combining the last two bounds, we obtain the desired estimate for
\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}
on
\tau\in(0,1/2].

In the case of
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{1}
for
\tau\in[1/2,1],
we use the same estimates as for
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}^{2}
, but with data at
\tau=1/2.
This allows us to obtain the desired estimates for
\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}
on
\tau\in[1/2,1].

∎

Remark 3.1
.

We notice that the only place in the above proof where we use an inequality that is not sharp is when bounding the
(\log\nabla)
operator using Lemma
2.7
. In order to prove top order estimates, we will take a different approach to avoid this issue in Section
3.3
.

Next, we prove the following lower order estimates for the regular components, establishing (
1.23
):

Proposition 3.3
.

Set
\eta=1/10.
For any
m<M
, we have the following estimate:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim

\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1+\eta}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

Using
\nabla_{\tau}
as a multiplier in the equations (
1.22
) and (
1.8
) satisfied by the regular quantities, we get:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}_{m}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

In the second estimate, we dropped the bulk term with a favorable sign obtained because
\sigma=1
in (
1.8
). Using the above two bounds, together with Lemma
3.2
and Gronwall, we obtain:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1+\eta}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We can estimate the last two error terms on the RHS as before, which implies the conclusion.
∎

In the above proof, we used the following bound on
\mathfrak{h}_{m}
:

Lemma 3.2
.

We have the estimate for any
m<M
:

\big\|\mathfrak{h}_{m}\big\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{H^{m+1}}+\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{m}}.

Proof.

We can write:

\mathfrak{h}_{m}=\nabla^{m}h-2(\log\nabla)\nabla^{m}\mathcal{O}=\nabla^{m}\mathfrak{h}-2[(\log\nabla),\nabla^{m}]\mathcal{O}.

Using (
2.15
) and (
2.16
) applied to the projection operator
P_{k}^{2},
we get for all
k\geq 0
:

\big\|[\nabla^{m},P_{k}^{2}]\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim 2^{-k}C\big(\|\not{Riem}_{0}\|_{H^{m}}\big)\cdot\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{m}}.

As a result, we have that:

\big\|[(\log\nabla),\nabla^{m}]\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim C\big(\|\not{Riem}_{0}\|_{H^{m}}\big)\cdot\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{m}}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{-k}\log 2^{k}.

Moreover, we notice that
\|\not{Riem}_{0}\|_{H^{M-1}}\leq C_{1}\lesssim 1
by our bounds on the background spacetime in Theorem
1.1
.
∎

We combine the above results to obtain the following lower order version of (
1.12
):

Corollary 3.1
.

We have the lower order estimate for
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}
, with
m<M:

\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big(1+|\log\tau|^{2}\big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1+\eta}}+\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{m+1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We also need estimates for the commutator term
\mathcal{C}=\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}-\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}
. To compute the equation satisfied by
\mathcal{C},
we first need to commute the equation of
\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}
using the commutation formula (
2.12
):

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)+\frac{1}{{\tau}}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}-4\Delta\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}=\psi\nabla\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}+

+O\Big(\big|\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|+\big|\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|+\big|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|+\big|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|\Big),

where we used (
1.5
) and (
1.6
). As a result, we obtain that
\mathcal{C}
satisfies the equation:

\displaystyle\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{C}\big)+\frac{1}{{\tau}}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{C}-4\Delta\mathcal{C}

\displaystyle=\psi\nabla\mathcal{C}+O\Big(\big|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{C}\big|\Big)+

\displaystyle+O\Big(\big|\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|+\big|\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|+\big|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|+\big|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big|\big).

Moreover, we notice that
\mathcal{C}
is a regular quantity at
\tau=0,
satisfying the expansion:

\mathcal{C}=2[\log\nabla,\nabla]\nabla^{M-1}\mathcal{O}+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{C}=O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\Big).

Note that as in the proof of Lemma
3.2
we get:

\big\|[\log\nabla,\nabla]\nabla^{M-1}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M}}.

We conclude the section by proving the estimate:

Proposition 3.4
.

The commutator term
\mathcal{C}=\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}-\nabla\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}
satisfies the following estimate:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{C}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\mathcal{C}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+\eta}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Additionally, for any
k\geq 0
and
\tau\in(0,2^{-k-1}]
we have:

\big\|\mathcal{C}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+\eta}}+2^{-k}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

Using the standard
\nabla_{\tau}
multiplier and the previous lower order estimates we get:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{C}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\mathcal{C}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+\eta}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

As before, we can also estimate
\big\|\mathcal{C}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}
in order to obtain the conclusion.
∎

3.2.2
Fractional estimates

In this section, we prove estimates in
H^{1/2}
for
\nabla(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0})_{Y}
and
\nabla_{\tau}(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0})_{Y}
. As explained in Section [Section 7]
1.1.3
, the error terms obtained in
[
Cic24
]
in the proof of the top order estimates for the singular component
\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}
can be simplified significantly using the fractional estimates proved in this section.

For the rest of the section we prove the following result:

Proposition 3.5
.

The singular component
(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0})_{Y}
satisfies the estimates:

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0})_{Y}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(1+|\log\tau|^{2}\big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2+\eta}}

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0})_{Y}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\tau^{2}}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2+\eta}}.

In order to prove this result we treat separately the components
\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1}
and
\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2}
. The above fractional estimates will be a consequence of Propositions
3.6
and
3.7
. We start with the component
\mathcal{A}:=\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{2},
which satisfies the equation:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big)+\frac{1}{{\tau}}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}-4\Delta\mathcal{A}=\psi\nabla\mathcal{A},

\mathcal{A}({\tau})=2(\log\nabla)\nabla^{M-1}\mathcal{O}+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}({\tau})=O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big).

Proposition 3.6
.

\mathcal{A}
satisfies the estimate:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2+\eta}}.

Proof.

For any
k\geq 0,
we apply
P_{k}
to the equation satisfied by
\mathcal{A}
to get:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big)+\frac{1}{\tau}P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}-4\Delta P_{k}\mathcal{A}=\psi\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}+[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\mathcal{A}+\psi[P_{k},\nabla]\mathcal{A}+[\nabla_{\tau},P_{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}.

We contract this equation with
P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}
and integrate by parts to obtain the energy estimate:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{4}]\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}

+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|[P_{k},\nabla]\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}.

We use Gronwall:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{4}]\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+

+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|[P_{k},\nabla]\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We use the bounds in Lemma
2.3
and Lemma
2.4
to control the commutation terms:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}2^{-2k}\big\|\mathcal{A}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}2^{-2k}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

As a result, we get that:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{-2k}\big\|\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+

+\int_{0}^{\tau}2^{-2k}\big\|\mathcal{A}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}2^{-2k}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We multiply by
2^{k}
and sum over all
k\geq 0.
This amounts to taking half of a derivative.

\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\mathcal{A}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We recall that from the standard lower order estimates we have:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+\eta}}.

Since
\sum_{k}P_{k}^{2}=I
, we obtain using our definition of fractional Sobolev spaces in Section
2
:

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+\eta}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{A}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2+\eta}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{A}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

Finally, we obtain the desired conclusion by Gronwall.
∎

Next, we consider the component
\mathcal{B}:=\big(\nabla^{M-1}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}^{1},
which satisfies the equation:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big)+\frac{1}{{\tau}}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}-4\Delta\mathcal{B}=\psi\nabla\mathcal{B},

\mathcal{B}({\tau})=2\nabla^{M-1}\mathcal{O}\log({\tau})+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}({\tau})=\frac{2\nabla^{M-1}\mathcal{O}}{\tau}+O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big).

Proposition 3.7
.

\mathcal{B}
satisfies the estimates:

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(1+|\log\tau|^{2}\big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}},

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\tau^{2}}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}.

Proof.

We denote
\mathcal{D}=\mathcal{B}/\log\tau.
Then
\mathcal{D}
satisfies the equation:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big)+\frac{1}{\tau}\bigg(1+\frac{2}{\log\tau}\bigg)\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}-4\Delta\mathcal{D}=\psi\nabla\mathcal{D},

\mathcal{D}({\tau})=2\nabla^{M-1}\mathcal{O}+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),\ \nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}({\tau})=O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big).

This has the same properties needed to do energy estimates as the equation satisfied by
\mathcal{A}.
Thus, an analogous proof gives for
\tau\in(0,1/2]
and any
k\geq 0
:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathcal{D}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}2^{-2k}\big\|\mathcal{D}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\mathcal{D}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}2^{-2k}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{\textbf{1}_{[1/10,1/2]}}{\tau^{\prime}|\log\tau^{\prime}|}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We point out that the error term
\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}|\log\tau^{\prime}|}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\cdot\textbf{1}_{[1/10,1/2]}
appears on the RHS because for
\tau\in[0,1/10]
we have
1+2/\log\tau\gtrsim 1.
This can be estimated as usual using Gronwall. We multiply by
2^{k}
and sum over
k:

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{D}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\mathcal{D}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{D}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We recall that from the standard lower order estimates we have for
\tau\in(0,1/2]
:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M}}.

Since
\sum_{k}P_{k}^{2}=I
, we obtain using our definition of fractional Sobolev spaces in Section
2
:

\displaystyle\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{D}|_{\tau=0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We obtain by Gronwall that for any
\tau\in(0,1/2]
:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{D}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}.

This implies that for any
\tau\in(0,1/2]
:

\big\|\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim|\log\tau|^{2}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}},

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim|\log\tau|^{2}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}+\frac{1}{\tau^{2}|\log\tau|^{2}}\big\|\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim|\log\tau|^{2}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}+\frac{1}{\tau^{2}}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M}}.

In particular, this also implies that:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\big|_{\tau=1/2}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\big|_{\tau=1/2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}.

Using the equation for
\mathcal{B}
on the time interval
\tau\in[1/2,1]
, we repeat the energy estimate that we did for
\mathcal{A}:

\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{B}\big|_{\tau=\frac{1}{2}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\big\|\mathcal{B}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla\mathcal{B}\big|_{\tau=\frac{1}{2}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+

+\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big|_{\tau=\frac{1}{2}}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\mathcal{B}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We recall that from the standard lower order estimates we have for
\tau\in[1/2,1]
:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M}}.

As a result, we have that for
\tau\in[1/2,1]
:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1/2}}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\int_{\frac{1}{2}}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla\mathcal{B}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}.

We combine the estimates for
\tau\in(0,1/2]
and
\tau\in[1/2,1]
to obtain the conclusion.
∎

3.3
Top order estimates

We prove top order estimates for the regular quantities
\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J},\nabla^{M}\Phi_{1},\ldots\nabla^{M}\Phi_{I}
as outlined in Section
1.1.3
. The analysis requires a precise understanding of the behavior of the
P_{k}
projections of each component. We treat separately the low frequency regime
\tau\in(0,2^{-k-1}]
in Section
3.3.1
and the high frequency regime
\tau\in[2^{-k-1},1]
in Section
3.3.2
. In Section
3.3.3
, we state the top order estimates of
[
Cic24
, Section 7]
for the singular component, and we explain their proof in analogy to the argument of the present paper. Finally, we combine the estimates in Section
3.3.4
to complete the proof of Theorem
1.1
.

In this section, we often work with the new time variable
t=2^{k}\tau
. We notice that we have a similar result to Lemma
3.1
, which allows us to control the error terms resulting from time derivatives of the metric and volume form. We note that for these terms we can apply Gronwall on the whole interval
t\in(0,2^{k}].

Lemma 3.3
.

For any smooth horizontal tensor
F
defined on
\mathcal{M},
we have:

\frac{1}{2}\frac{d}{dt}\big\|F\big\|_{L^{2}(S_{t})}^{2}=\int_{S_{t}}F\cdot\nabla_{t}FdVol_{\not{g}}+O\Big(2^{-2k}t\big\|F\big\|_{L^{2}(S_{t})}^{2}\Big).

Proof.

The proof follows from Lemma
3.1
, since
\partial_{t}=2^{-k}\partial_{\tau}.

∎

3.3.1
Low frequency regime estimates

We prove a low frequency regime estimate for the regular components
P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J},P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{1},\ldots,P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{I}
, for each
k\geq 0
. The main idea is to propagate for
\tau\in(0,2^{-k-1}]
the
L^{2}
bounds satisfied by the asymptotic data at
\mathcal{I}^{-}
, using
\nabla_{\tau}
as a multiplier.

Proposition 3.8
.

For any
k\geq 0
and
\tau\leq 2^{-k-1}
, we have the estimate:

\displaystyle\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\lesssim

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\nabla P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\bigg(\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

We denote
\xi_{0}=\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}
and for

1\leq i\leq I
we denote
\xi_{i}=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}
. We introduce the new time variable
t=2^{k}\tau
. Equations (
1.8
) and (
1.22
) with
\sigma=1,m=M
can be written as follows for all
0\leq i\leq I
:

\nabla_{t}\big(\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big)+\frac{1}{t}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}-\frac{4}{2^{2k}}\cdot\Delta\xi_{i}=\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\xi_{j}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot F_{M}^{i}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}.

For any
k\geq 0,
we apply
P_{k}
to each equation:

\displaystyle\nabla_{t}\big(P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big)+\frac{1}{t}P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}-\frac{4}{2^{2k}}\cdot\Delta P_{k}\xi_{i}=\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla P_{k}\xi_{j}+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\psi[P_{k},\nabla]\xi_{j}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi_{j}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot P_{k}F_{M}^{i}+[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big).

We contract each equation with
P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}
and integrate by parts to obtain the energy estimate:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{t}]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{t}]P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[P_{k},\nabla]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}\bigg)dt^{\prime}.

We note that we dropped the bulk term with a favorable sign in the above estimate. We use Gronwall for
t\in\big[0,1/2\big]:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{t}]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{t}]P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{4k}}\big\|[P_{k},\nabla]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{4k}}\big\|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\big\|[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{4k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{4k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We use Lemma
2.3
in order to bound the commutation terms. Thus, we get:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{6k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{4k}}\big\|\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{4k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{4k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We change variables to
\tau
and get for all
\tau\in[0,2^{-k-1}]:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Next, using Lemma
3.1
and (
2.22
), we also have the bound for all
\tau\in[0,2^{-k-1}]:

\displaystyle\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{-k}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{-k}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{-5k}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using Gronwall and the previous estimate, we conclude that:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

∎

3.3.2
High frequency regime estimates

We prove a high frequency regime estimate for the regular components
P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J},P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{1},\ldots,P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{I}
, for each
k\geq 0
. The idea of the proof is to use
2^{k}\tau\nabla_{\tau}
as a multiplier for
\tau\in[2^{-k-1},1].
As in the previous section, our estimates are simplified by the presence of bulk terms with favorable signs. We also notice that the same argument applies to prove the high frequency regime estimate for the singular component
P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}
, see already Section
3.3.3
.

Proposition 3.9
.

For any
k\geq 0,\ \tau\in\big[2^{-k-1},1\big]
we have the estimate for the regular components:

\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\frac{1}{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\frac{1}{2^{k}}\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{\tau=2^{-k-1}}+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{\tau=2^{-k-1}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\bigg((\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla^{M+1}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}

+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Proof.

We denote
\xi_{0}=\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}
and for

1\leq i\leq I
we denote
\xi_{i}=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}
. We introduce the new time variable
t=2^{k}\tau
. As before, equations (
1.8
) and (
1.22
) with
\sigma=1,m=M
can be written for all
0\leq i\leq I
:

\nabla_{t}\big(\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big)+\frac{1}{t}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}-\frac{4}{2^{2k}}\cdot\Delta\xi_{i}=\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\xi_{j}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot F_{M}^{i}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}.

We multiply by
\sqrt{t}
to get for all
0\leq i\leq I
:

\nabla_{t}\big(\nabla_{t}(\xi_{i}\sqrt{t})\big)+\frac{1}{4t^{2}}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}-\frac{4}{2^{2k}}\cdot\Delta\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}=\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\xi_{j}\sqrt{t}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot F_{M}^{i}\sqrt{t}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\sqrt{t}.

For any
k\geq 0
, we apply
P_{k}
to obtain the equations for all
0\leq i\leq I
:

\nabla_{t}\big(P_{k}\nabla_{t}(\xi_{i}\sqrt{t})\big)+\frac{1}{4t^{2}}P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}-\frac{4}{2^{2k}}\Delta P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}=\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\psi\nabla P_{k}\xi_{j}\sqrt{t}+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\psi[P_{k},\nabla]\xi_{j}\sqrt{t}+

+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi_{j}\sqrt{t}+\frac{P_{k}\big(F_{M}^{i}\sqrt{t}+\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\sqrt{t}\big)}{2^{2k}}+[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}

We contract each equation with
P_{k}\nabla_{t}(\xi_{i}\sqrt{t})
and integrate by parts to obtain the following energy estimate:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{(t^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=1/2}+

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{(t^{\prime})^{2}}\big|P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[P_{k},\nabla_{t}]\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{t}]\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[\nabla,\nabla_{t}]P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+

+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[P_{k},\nabla]\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|+\int_{1/2}^{t}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|P_{k}\big(F_{M}^{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}+\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big)\big|.

We point out that the bulk term with a favorable sign provides a significant simplification for our analysis. On the other hand, in the analysis of the second model system the corresponding term will create several complications. We use Gronwall for
t\in\big[1/2,2^{k}\big],
and the bounds in Lemma
2.4
to get for all
0\leq i\leq I
:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=1/2}

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}t^{\prime}}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\Big(\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big)+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{t^{\prime}}{2^{4k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{H^{1}}\bigg)+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{H^{1}}+

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{t^{\prime}}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big(\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big)+

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}

Once again we use Gronwall for
t\in\big[1/2,2^{k}\big]
to get for all
0\leq i\leq I
:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=1/2}

+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{3k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

+\sum_{j=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{5k}}\big\|\xi_{j}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

Finally, we sum the above estimates for all
0\leq i\leq I
to obtain:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}t\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{1}{t}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{t}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=1/2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{3k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{1}{2^{5k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\nabla_{t}\xi_{i}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{t^{\prime}}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{1/2}^{t}\frac{t^{\prime}}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We change variables to
\tau
in order to obtain the conclusion.
∎

3.3.3
Singular component estimates

The singular component decouples from the rest of the system, so it can be studied separately from the regular quantities. Relying on the lower order estimates for the singular component in Section
3.2
, the proof of the top order estimates is done in
[
Cic24
, Theorem 7.2]
, where we prove:

\displaystyle\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1}},

(3.3)

\displaystyle\sum_{m=0}^{M}\big\|\big(\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big(1+|\log\tau|^{2}\big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1}}.

(3.4)

We outline the strategy of this proof, which follows similar steps to the above argument for the regular components. In the high frequency regime, the estimate is entirely analogous to the one proved in Proposition
3.9
. Moreover, we point out that the only error terms on the right hand side come from the singular component itself, since there are no inhomogeneous terms in (
1.21
). Additionally, once we prove (
3.3
) and (
3.4
), we also have the high frequency estimate:

\sum_{\tau>2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}

(3.5)

In the low frequency regime we take a similar approach to the regular components. However, we need to account for the singular behavior of the solution at
\tau=0,
which can be seen in the expansions:

\displaystyle P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}({\tau})=2P_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}\log({2^{k}\tau})+R_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}+O\big({\tau}^{2}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big),

\displaystyle P_{k}\nabla_{2^{-k}\partial_{\tau}}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}({\tau})=2P_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}\frac{1}{2^{k}\tau}+O\big({\tau}|\log({\tau})|^{2}\big).

We notice that
R_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}
on the right hand side is defined at
\tau=0
and extended by Lie transport in time, according to Lemma
2.9
. The strategy is to prove estimates on the equation for
P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}/\log t
where
t=2^{k}\tau.
It is essential to subtract off the term with
R_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O},
which is lower order in terms of angular derivatives by Lemma
2.8
. We also use the lower order estimates in Section
3.2
in order to deal with the error terms arising from commutation.

Finally, we combine the low frequency and high frequency regime estimates for the singular component in order to obtain (
3.3
) and (
3.4
), similarly to the approach used in the next section.

3.3.4
The proof of Theorem
1.1

In this section, we combine the low frequency regime and the high frequency regime estimates for the regular components to establish top order estimates. Together with (
3.3
), (
3.4
), and the lower order estimates, these complete the proof of Theorem
1.1
.

Proof of Theorem
1.1
.

Using (
3.3
), (
3.4
), and the lower order estimates in Propositions
3.2
and
3.3
, we notice that in order to establish Theorem
1.1
it suffices to prove the estimate for all
\tau\in(0,1]:

\tau\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\tau\big\|\nabla(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\tau\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\tau\big\|\nabla^{M+1}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\big\|\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\Phi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

For the rest of the proof we show this estimate. The first step is to combine the high frequency regime estimate in Proposition
3.9
with the low frequency regime estimate in Proposition
3.8
. We denote
\xi_{0}=\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{J}
and
\xi_{i}=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{i}
for
1\leq i\leq I
. Using the high frequency regime estimate in Proposition
3.9
, we get that for
k\geq 0
and
\tau\in\big[2^{-k-1},1\big]
:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{2^{k}}{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\bigg(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{\tau=2^{-k-1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2^{k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We use the low frequency regime estimate in Proposition
3.8
and the bound (
3.4
) for the singular component. Thus, we get for
k\geq 0
and
\tau\in\big[2^{-k-1},1\big]
the following high frequency regime estimate:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\frac{2^{k}}{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{2^{-k-1}}\frac{1}{2^{3k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{2^{-k-1}}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{2^{-k-1}}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\bigg(2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2^{k}}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The second step is to prove a bound for the sum of the non-negative frequencies. We define the following energy for all
k\geq 0
:

2^{k}E_{k}^{2}(\tau)=\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}2^{k}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

Using the singular component high frequency regime estimate (
3.5
), we get that for all
k\geq 0
:

\sum_{\tau>2^{-k-1}}\int_{2^{-k-1}}^{\tau}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}>2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\big(\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big)_{Y}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}.

As a result, the above high frequency regime estimate implies the bound:

\sum_{\tau>2^{-k-1}}2^{k}E_{k}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+

+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\sum_{k\geq 0}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big(2^{k}\tau^{\prime}+2^{-k}\big)\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The low frequency estimates in Proposition
3.3
and (
3.4
) imply the bound:

\sum_{\tau\leq 2^{-k-1}}2^{k}E_{k}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\sum_{k\geq 0}\int_{0}^{\tau}\frac{1}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

As a result, we completed the second step of the proof and showed that:

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k}E_{k}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

The final step of the proof is dealing with the negative frequencies
k<0.
We remark that we can repeat the proof of Proposition
3.3
for
m=M
and use (
3.4
) in order to deal with the singular component. Thus, to get:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We notice that by
[
KR06
]
, we have that
\|P_{k}\nabla F\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim 2^{k}\|F\|_{L^{2}}
for any
k<0
. We obtain the following bound for the negative frequencies:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}\sum_{k<0}\Big(2^{k}\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{k}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\lesssim\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}

\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|^{2}_{H^{M+1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

To conclude the proof of Theorem
1.1
, we combine the estimates proved for non-negative frequencies and negative frequencies. We then apply Gronwall to obtain:

\sum_{i=0}^{I}\tau\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\tau\big\|\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1/2}}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}^{0}\big\|^{2}_{H^{1}}+\sum_{i=0}^{I}\int_{0}^{\tau}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}.

∎

4
Estimates for the Second Model System

In this section we prove Theorem
1.2
, obtaining estimates for solutions of the second model system at
\tau\in(0,1)
in terms of the solution at
\tau=1.
We also prove estimates for the asymptotic data at
\mathcal{I}^{-}.
We follow the steps outlined in the Section
1.1.4
of the Introduction, and we advise the reader to refer to this section for assistance while reading the proof below.

To complete the statement of Theorem
1.2
, we first define in detail the energy
\mathcal{E}_{II}
of the solution on
S_{\tau}:

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{II}(\tau)=

\displaystyle\tau\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{M+1/2}}^{2}+\tau^{2}\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{M+3/2}}^{2}+\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{M+3/2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\sum_{m=0}^{M}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\sum_{m=0}^{M}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.1)

In Section
4.1
, we prove estimates for the regular quantities
\Phi_{1},\ldots,\Phi_{I}
which satisfy the equations (
1.8
) with
\sigma=2
and decouple from the singular quantity
\Phi_{0}.
We prove (
1.25
) and (
1.26
) in Proposition
4.1
. In Section
4.2
, we prove the preliminary bound (
1.27
) in Proposition
4.2
. In Section
4.3
, we prove the low frequency regime estimates in Propositions
4.3
and
4.4
. In Section
4.4
, we prove the preliminary high frequency regime estimate in Proposition
4.5
. We improve this in Section
4.5
to obtain the high frequency regime estimate in Proposition
4.6
. Combining the previous estimates in Section
4.6
, we complete the proof of the main estimate (
1.13
) in Theorem
1.2
. Finally, we establish the estimates (
1.14
) and (
1.15
) for the asymptotic data at
\mathcal{I}^{-}
in Section
4.7
. We prove (
1.28
) in Proposition
4.7
and (
1.29
) in Proposition
4.8
.

Notation.
Unless otherwise noted, in this section we write
A\lesssim B
for some quantities
A,B>0
if there exists a constant
C>0
depending only on the constants
M,C_{0},C_{2}
defined in the Introduction, such that
A\leq CB.

We point out that the main difficulty in our argument is dealing with the top order quantity
\xi=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}.
For this part we use as a guideline the toy problem considered in
[
Cic24
, Section 9]
, where we studied the equation satisfied by
\xi=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0},
but we dropped the terms
F^{\prime}_{M}=F_{M}^{0}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\psi\nabla^{M+1}\Phi_{i}
for simplicity.

4.1
Estimates for the regular quantities

In this section, we prove the main estimates for all the regular quantities
\Phi_{i}
, with
1\leq i\leq I:

Proposition 4.1
.

For all
0\leq m\leq M
and
1\leq i\leq I
we have:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{I}\bigg(\big\|\Phi_{j}\big\|_{H^{m+3/2}}^{2}\big|_{\tau=1}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\Phi_{j}\big\|_{H^{m+1/2}}^{2}\big|_{\tau=1}+\sum_{k=0}^{m}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{k}^{j}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\bigg)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau).

Proof.

For the purpose of this proof we fix
0\leq m\leq M
and we denote
\xi_{i}=\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}.
We can rewrite (
1.8
) with
\sigma=2
for all
1\leq i\leq I:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big)-\frac{1}{\tau}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}-4\Delta\xi_{i}=\sum_{j=1}^{I}\psi\nabla\xi_{j}+F_{m}^{i}.

(4.2)

Preliminary estimates.

We first prove the following preliminary estimates for all
1\leq i\leq I:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\sum_{j=1}^{I}\bigg(\big\|\Phi_{j}\big\|_{H^{m+1}}^{2}\big|_{\tau=1}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\Phi_{j}\big\|_{H^{m}}^{2}\big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{m}^{j}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg).

To prove this, we contract each equation (
4.2
) by
\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}.
Using Lemma
3.1
, we obtain the energy estimate:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\nabla\xi_{j}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use Cauchy-Schwarz, Gronwall, and the bulk term to obtain:

\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\nabla\xi_{j}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

On the other hand, we also have the bound:

\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We sum the last two inequalities for all
1\leq i\leq I:

\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{I}\Big(\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use Gronwall to complete the proof of the above preliminary estimate. For the data terms at
\tau=1
we also use the commutation formula (
2.12
) and the assumption on the background spacetime
\|\psi\|_{H^{M+1}(S_{1})}\leq C_{2}
.

The main estimates.

For each
k\geq 0
, we apply
P_{k}
to equation (
4.2
) to obtain:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big)-\frac{1}{\tau}P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}-4\Delta P_{k}\xi_{i}=\sum_{j=1}^{I}P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\xi_{j}\big)+P_{k}F_{m}^{i}+[\nabla_{\tau},P_{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}.

We contract each equation by
P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i},
in order to obtain the energy estimate:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{4}]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}

+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|P_{k}(\psi\nabla\xi_{j})\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|P_{k}F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|[\nabla_{\tau},P_{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use Cauchy-Schwarz and Gronwall to obtain:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{4}]\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi_{j}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|P_{k}\nabla\xi_{j}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use Lemma
2.3
and Lemma
2.4
to get:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{-2k}\big\|\xi_{j}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{j=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|P_{k}\nabla\xi_{j}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{-2k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We multiply each inequality by
2^{k}
and sum for all
1\leq i\leq I:

\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{k}}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{-k}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}2^{k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{-k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We then sum for all
k\geq 0,
using the preliminary estimate as well to obtain:

\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\sum_{i=1}^{I}\Big(\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{m}^{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{i=1}^{I}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{i}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use Gronwall and we bound the initial data term at
\tau=1
as before (using
C_{2}
) to obtain the conclusion.
∎

4.2
Preliminary estimates for the singular quantities

For every
0\leq m\leq M
, we write the equation (
1.7
) for
\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}
as:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big)+\frac{1}{\tau}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}-4\Delta\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}=\psi\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{0}+F_{m}^{\prime},

(4.3)

where we denote
F_{m}^{\prime}=\psi\sum_{i=1}^{I}\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{i}+F_{m}^{0}.
We prove the preliminary estimate (
1.27
) as outlined in Section
1.1.4
of the Introduction:

Proposition 4.2
.

For all
0\leq m\leq M
and
\tau\in[0,1]
we have the estimates:

\displaystyle\big\|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\tau\big\|\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{m+3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{m+1/2}}^{2}\bigg)\bigg|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

For the purpose of this proof, we denote
\xi_{m}=\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}.
We can rewrite equation (
4.3
) as:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big)-4\tau\Delta\xi_{m}=\tau\psi\nabla\xi_{m}+\tau F_{m}^{\prime}.

Using Lemma
3.1
, we obtain the energy estimate:

\big\|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}
