+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big(\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}+(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using Gronwall, we obtain:

\big\|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{4}\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Similarly, we have the estimate:

\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

In particular, this implies:

\tau^{4}\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{4}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime},

which gives in the above inequality:

\big\|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{4}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

By Gronwall, we obtain:

\big\|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using this, we also have:

\tau\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}\lesssim\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\sqrt{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3/2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\lesssim\Big(\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\bigg)\Big|_{\tau=1}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\sqrt{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\xi_{m}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We obtain the conclusion using Gronwall and bounding the initial data term at
\tau=1
.
∎

4.3
Low frequency regime estimates

For the rest of Section
4
, we denote the top order term
\xi=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}.
We write (
1.7
) as:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big)+\frac{1}{\tau}\nabla_{\tau}\xi-4\Delta\xi=\psi\nabla\xi+F_{M}^{\prime},

(4.4)

where we denote
F_{M}^{\prime}=\psi\sum_{i=1}^{I}\nabla^{M+1}\Phi_{i}+F_{M}^{0}.

As outlined in Section
1.1.4
of the Introduction, we consider
X=2^{x+1}
to be a large constant, to be chosen later depending only on
M,C_{0},C_{2}.
We consider the following regimes:

•

Negative frequencies
k<0
;

•

Low frequency regime
0\leq k<x
for all
\tau\in[0,1]
;

•

Low frequency regime
k\geq x
for
\tau\in[0,X2^{-k-1}]
;

•

High frequency regime
k\geq x
for
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
.

Notation.
In addition to our previous notation convention, we write
A\lesssim_{X}B
for some quantities
A,B>0
if there exists a constant
C>0
depending only on the constants
M,C_{0},C_{2}
and
X
, such that
A\leq CB.
Otherwise, if we write
A\lesssim B
then the implicit constant
C
is independent of
X
.

In order to deal with the negative frequencies, we can simply use the preliminary estimate (
1.27
). Similarly, we have the following bound in the low frequency regime
0\leq k<x
:

Proposition 4.3
.

For
0\leq k<x
, we have the low frequency regime estimate for all
\tau\in[0,1]
:

\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim_{X}2^{-3k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-3k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

Since
0\leq k<x,
we have using the preliminary estimate in Proposition
4.2
:

\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim_{X}2^{-3k}\Big(\big\|\tau\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)\lesssim_{X}2^{-3k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-3k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

∎

We prove the main estimate in the low frequency regime
k\geq x
for
\tau\in[0,X2^{-k-1}]
. The idea is to prove a similar estimate to (
1.27
) for
P_{k}\xi.
We note that we follow the argument for the toy problem in
[
Cic24
, Section 9]
, while keeping track of the inhomogeneous terms.

Proposition 4.4
.

For any
0\leq\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1
we have the low frequency regime estimate:

\displaystyle\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle X^{2}2^{-2k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\Big|_{\tau=X2^{-k-1}}+C_{X}2^{-3k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+

\displaystyle+C_{X}2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+C_{X}2^{-3k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

We apply
P_{k}
to (
4.4
) to obtain for any
k\geq x
:

\nabla_{\tau}\big(\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big)-4\tau\Delta P_{k}\xi=\tau P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\xi\big)+\tau P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}+[\nabla_{\tau},P_{k}]\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi.

We contract the equation with
\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi
and integrate by parts to obtain the following energy estimate:

\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim

\lesssim X^{2}2^{-2k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\Big|_{X2^{-k-1}}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{4}]\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[\nabla,\nabla_{4}]P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\xi\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|[P_{k},\nabla_{4}]\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We point out that similarly to the proof of Proposition
4.2
, we obtain a bulk term with a favorable sign, which we drop. We use Lemma
2.3
to bound the commutation terms:

\displaystyle\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim X^{2}2^{-2k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\Big|_{X2^{-k-1}}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}2^{-k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}d\tau^{\prime}+

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using Cauchy-Schwarz, we obtain the bound:

\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim X^{2}2^{-2k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\Big|_{X2^{-k-1}}+

+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}\frac{2^{k}}{X}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}\frac{2^{k}}{X}\big\|\tau^{\prime}P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+C_{X}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{-3k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

+C_{X}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{-k}(\tau^{\prime})^{4}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+2^{-k}C_{X}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using the Gronwall inequality for
\tau\leq X2^{-k-1}\leq 1
we get:

\big\|\tau\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim X^{2}2^{-2k}\Big(\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\Big)\Big|_{X2^{-k-1}}+

+C_{X}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{-3k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+C_{X}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{-k}(\tau^{\prime})^{4}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{-k}C_{X}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Finally, we use the above estimate (
1.27
) to complete the proof.
∎

4.4
High frequency regime estimates

In this section, we prove a high frequency regime estimate for the top order term
\xi=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}.
The proof is similar to that of Proposition
3.9
, but in the current case the bulk term has an unfavorable sign, which will create several complications in Section
4.5
. Moreover, it is essential that the implicit constant in the estimate obtained is independent of the parameter
X.

Proposition 4.5
.

\xi
satisfies the high frequency regime estimate for any
\tau\in\big[X2^{-k-1},1\big]:

2^{k}\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{2^{k}}{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{k}\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim\bigg(2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+2^{k}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{\tau=1}

+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{\tau^{\prime}}{2^{k}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F^{\prime}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Proof.

We introduce the new time variable
t=X^{-1}2^{k}\tau\in[1/2,X^{-1}2^{k}]
. The equation (
4.4
) becomes:

\nabla_{t}\big(\nabla_{t}\xi\big)+\frac{1}{t}\nabla_{t}\xi-4\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\cdot\Delta\xi=\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\xi+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\cdot F_{M}^{\prime}.

We multiply by
\sqrt{t}
to get:

\nabla_{t}\big(\nabla_{t}(\xi\sqrt{t})\big)+\frac{1}{4t^{2}}\xi\sqrt{t}-4\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\cdot\Delta\xi\sqrt{t}=\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\cdot\psi\nabla\xi\sqrt{t}+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\cdot F_{M}^{\prime}\sqrt{t}.

We apply
P_{k}
to the equation:

\displaystyle\nabla_{t}\big(P_{k}\nabla_{t}(\xi\sqrt{t})\big)+\frac{1}{4t^{2}}P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}-4\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\Delta P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}=

\displaystyle\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\psi\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\psi[P_{k},\nabla]\xi\sqrt{t}+

\displaystyle+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi\sqrt{t}+\frac{X^{2}P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\sqrt{t}}{2^{2k}}+[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t}.

We contract the equation with
P_{k}\nabla_{t}(\xi\sqrt{t})
and integrate by parts. We notice that using the analogue of Lemma
3.3
for the new time variable
t
does not introduce any constants that depend on
X.
We obtain the energy estimate:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\bigg(X^{-1}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=X^{-1}2^{k}}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{1}{(t^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{1}{(t^{\prime})^{2}}\big|P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big|\cdot\big|[P_{k},\nabla_{t}]\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|\nabla[P_{k},\nabla_{t}]\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[\nabla,\nabla_{t}]P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[P_{k},\nabla]\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[P_{k},\psi]\nabla\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\int_{S^{n}}\big|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|\cdot\big|[\nabla_{t},P_{k}]\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big|dt^{\prime}.

We use Lemma
2.4
, and Gronwall for
t\in\big[1/2,X^{-1}2^{k}\big]
(to deal with the fifth and sixth error terms):

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\bigg(X^{-1}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=X^{-1}2^{k}}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{1}{(t^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}t^{\prime}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\Big(\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big)dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{4}t^{\prime}}{2^{4k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\bigg(\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{H^{1}}\bigg)+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{2}t^{\prime}}{2^{2k}}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big(\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}+2^{-k}\big\|\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}\Big)dt^{\prime}.

We use Gronwall for
t\in\big[1/2,X^{-1}2^{k}\big]
to get:

\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{t^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X^{2}}{2^{2k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\sqrt{t}\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\lesssim

\lesssim\bigg(X^{-1}2^{k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X}{2^{k}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{X}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|_{t=X^{-1}2^{k}}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{1}{(t^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{3}}{2^{3k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{5}(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{3}(t^{\prime})^{2}}{2^{3k}}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{t}\xi\sqrt{t^{\prime}}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+

+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{3}t^{\prime}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{3}(t^{\prime})^{3}}{2^{5k}}\big\|\nabla_{t}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}+\int_{t}^{X^{-1}2^{k}}\frac{X^{3}t^{\prime}}{2^{3k}}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}dt^{\prime}.

We change variables to
\tau
and we obtain the conclusion.
∎

4.5
Improved high frequency estimates

The goal of this section is to improve the high frequency regime estimate of Proposition
4.5
in order to prove estimates in the high frequency regime only in terms of data and the inhomogeneous terms.

Proposition 4.6
.

We denote by
\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}
the characteristic function of
\{1\geq\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}\},
and we denote:

a_{k}(\tau):=\tau\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\frac{1}{\tau}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}+\tau\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|^{2}_{L^{2}}.

We have the improved high frequency estimate for any
\tau\in(0,1]
:

\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.5)

The rest of this section is dedicated to the proof of this proposition. We follow the detailed outline of the proof from Section
1.1.4
of the Introduction, and we divide the proof into multiple steps. We note that the proof is very similar to the toy problem considered in
[
Cic24
, Section 9]
, but in the current case we also need to keep track of the inhomogeneous terms. Additionally, in the present proof we fill in several of the details that we omitted in
[
Cic24
]
, such as the proof of the Gronwall-like inequality in Lemma
4.1
.

Throughout the proof we use the schematic notation
\{d_{k}\}_{k\geq 0}
for data terms at
\tau=1
which satisfy:

\sum_{k\geq 0}d_{k}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}.

Consequences of Proposition
4.5
.

The starting point is the preliminary high frequency regime estimate in Proposition
4.5
. Using the above notation, we have for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\lesssim 2^{k}a_{k}(1)+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\overline{e_{k}}(\tau^{\prime})+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

where we define the energies:

\displaystyle e_{k}

\displaystyle=2^{k}\tau^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{k}\tau^{3}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

\displaystyle\overline{e_{k}}

\displaystyle=2^{k}\tau\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{\tau}{2^{k}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\frac{\tau^{3}}{2^{k}}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

The error term containing
e_{k}
is similar to the error terms with different projection operators of Section
3
, as explained in Section
1.1.4
. We deal with these terms towards the end of our argument, when summing the estimates obtained for all
k\geq x.
In the meantime we simply keep track of these terms, similarly to the inhomogeneous terms.

On the other hand, we can already bound the error terms containing
\overline{e_{k}}
using the preliminary estimate in Proposition
4.2
. We can bound the first term in
\overline{e_{k}}
by the second term, using the finite band property for LP projections. We then apply (
1.27
) to get:

\int_{\tau}^{1}\overline{e_{k}}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim_{X}d_{k}+2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

As a consequence, we proved that for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
we have the high frequency regime estimate:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\lesssim C_{X}d_{k}+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})+C_{X}2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Applying the refined Poincaré inequality.

According to Section
1.1.4
of the Introduction, the second error term in the above estimate causes significant challenges. We also explained in Section
1.1.2
that we could not bound this term directly using the finite band property for LP projections. Instead, we use the refined Poincaré inequality for LP projections (
2.28
). As a result, we have for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
and
\delta>0
:

\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{-k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\delta\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\frac{1}{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{-3k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

The last term in this inequality is bounded using the preliminary estimate (
1.27
):

\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{-3k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim_{X}2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

As a result, there exist constants
C^{\prime},C_{X},C_{X,\delta}>0
such that for all
k\geq x
and
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\leq C_{X,\delta}d_{k}+\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{-2k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\cdot 2^{k}a_{k}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+C^{\prime}\delta\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+

+C_{X,\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+C_{X,\delta}2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+C_{X,\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We can deal with the second error term by using Gronwall for
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
. We compute:

\exp\bigg(\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{-2k}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}d\tau^{\prime}\bigg)\leq\exp\bigg(\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta}\cdot\frac{1}{X^{2}}\bigg)\leq 2,

where we fix
X>0
large enough, depending on
C^{\prime}
and
\delta
, such that:

\frac{C^{\prime}}{\delta}\cdot\frac{1}{X^{2}}\leq\log(2).

(4.6)

Therefore, we have for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\leq C_{\delta}d_{k}+2C^{\prime}\delta\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C_{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})+C_{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{-k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Bounding the low frequency regime error terms.

As a consequence of the refined Poincaré inequality, the second term in the above has both a low frequency regime and a high frequency regime part. We want to separate these two, and apply the low frequency regime estimates in Section
4.3
. This process creates a sum of discrete error terms which pose additional difficulties. We notice that we can write:

\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}2^{-8k+7l}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\leq\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-8k+6l}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}+

+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{4}}\sum_{l=0}^{x-1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{4}}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime},

where the first term is in the high frequency regime and the last two terms are in the low frequency regime. We use Proposition
4.3
to obtain:

\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{4}}\sum_{l=0}^{x-1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim_{X}\mathcal{D}_{II}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\frac{2^{-8k}}{\tau^{7}}+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\frac{2^{-8k}}{\tau^{7}}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim_{X}d_{k}+2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Similarly, we use Proposition
4.4
to obtain:

\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{4}}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}=\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-l-1}}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{4}}2^{-8k+7l}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}

\lesssim\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}X\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}\frac{2^{-8k+6l}}{\tau^{3}}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})+C_{X}\mathcal{D}_{II}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\frac{2^{-8k}}{\tau^{7}}+

+C_{X}\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\frac{2^{-8k}}{\tau^{7}}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+C_{X}\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\frac{2^{-8k}}{\tau^{7}}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We notice that in the above estimate the last term is obtained by bounding:

\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-l-1}}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{4}}2^{-8k+6l}\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{X2^{-l-1}}\tilde{\tau}^{2}\big\|P_{l}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tilde{\tau}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim_{X}\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}\frac{1}{\tau^{3}}2^{-8k+4l}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tilde{\tau}2^{l}\big\|P_{l}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tilde{\tau}.

As a result, we proved that there exist constants
C^{\prime\prime},C_{\delta}>0
such that for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\leq C_{\delta}d_{k}+C^{\prime\prime}\delta\cdot\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-8k+6l}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}+C^{\prime\prime}\delta X\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}\frac{2^{-8k+6l}}{\tau^{3}}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})

+C_{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+C_{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+C_{\delta}2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+C_{\delta}2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Gronwall-like inequality.

Our next goal is to deal with the second term in the above estimate using a suitable Gronwall-like inequality. We set-up the problem in order to isolate this error term. We consider
\delta>0
to be small enough, so that:

C^{\prime\prime}\delta<\frac{1}{10}.

(4.7)

We introduce some notation for the error terms on the right hand side of the above estimate:

\displaystyle S_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}C^{\prime\prime}\delta X\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}\frac{2^{-8k+6l}}{\tau^{3}}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1}),

\displaystyle E_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}C_{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime},

\displaystyle I_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle=\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}C_{\delta}2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}C_{\delta}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime},

\displaystyle A_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle=C_{\delta}d_{k}+S_{k}(\tau)+E_{k}(\tau)+I_{k}(\tau).

Using this notation, we have that for all
k\geq x
and
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\leq A_{k}(\tau)+\frac{1}{10}\cdot\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-8k+6l}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.8)

This motivates us to prove the following Gronwall-like lemma:

Lemma 4.1
.

We consider the functions
u,A,b,c:\mathbb{N}\times[0,1]\rightarrow[0,\infty),
which for all
k\geq x,\ \tau\in(0,1]
satisfy the inequality:

u(k,\tau)\leq A(k,\tau)+b(k)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}c(l,\tau^{\prime})u(l,\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}

(4.9)

Then, we have that for all
k\geq x,\ \tau\in(0,1]
:

u(k,\tau)\leq A(k,\tau)+b(k)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}c(l,\tau^{\prime})A(l,\tau^{\prime})\prod_{j=l+1}^{k-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tau^{\prime\prime})d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.10)

Proof.

We prove this by induction on
k.
In the case
k=x,
we have from (
4.9
) that
u(x,\tau)\leq A(x,\tau)
as desired. Next, we assume that (
4.10
) holds for
x,x+1,\ldots,k
and we prove it for
k+1.
We have:

u(k+1,\tau)\leq A(k+1,\tau)+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})u(l,\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}

\leq A(k+1,\tau)+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})\bigg\{A(l,\tau^{\prime})+b(l)\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\sum_{i=x}^{l-1}c(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})A(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})\bigg)d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg\}d\tau^{\prime}

\leq A(k+1,\tau)+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})A(l,\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+

+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k}\sum_{i=x}^{l-1}\bigg\{c(l,\tau^{\prime})b(l)c(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})A(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})d\tilde{\tau}\bigg)\bigg\}d\tau^{\prime\prime}d\tau^{\prime}

\leq A(k+1,\tau)+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})A(l,\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+

+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{i=x}^{k-1}\sum_{l=i+1}^{k}\bigg\{c(l,\tau^{\prime})b(l)c(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})A(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})d\tilde{\tau}\bigg)\bigg\}d\tau^{\prime}d\tau^{\prime\prime}

\leq A(k+1,\tau)+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})A(l,\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+

+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{i=x}^{k-1}c(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})A(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})\bigg\{\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{l=i+1}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})b(l)\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})d\tilde{\tau}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}\bigg\}d\tau^{\prime\prime}

\leq A(k+1,\tau)+b(k+1)\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{i=x}^{k}c(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})A(i,\tau^{\prime\prime})\bigg\{1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}\sum_{l=i+1}^{k}c(l,\tau^{\prime})b(l)\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})d\tilde{\tau}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}\bigg\}d\tau^{\prime\prime}

We conclude, since we have:

1+\sum_{l=i+1}^{k}\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}c(l,\tau^{\prime})b(l)d\tau^{\prime}\cdot\prod_{j=i+1}^{l-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})d\tilde{\tau}\bigg)=\prod_{j=i+1}^{k}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tilde{\tau})d\tilde{\tau}\bigg).

∎

Consequences of Lemma
4.1
.

We use the Gronwall lemma established above for the inequality (
4.8
).

Corollary 4.1
.

For all
k\geq x
and
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
, we have the high frequency regime estimate:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\lesssim A_{k}(\tau)+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=0}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\cdot A_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}

(4.11)

Proof.

We apply Lemma
4.1
for the inequality (
4.8
), where:

u(k,\tau)=2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau},\ b(k)=\frac{1}{10}\cdot 2^{-8k},\ c(k,\tau)=\tau^{-3}2^{6k}\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}.

From (
4.10
) we obtain for
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\leq A_{k}(\tau)+2^{-8k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}A_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\cdot\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}2^{6l}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}\prod_{j=l+1}^{k-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tau^{\prime\prime})d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}.

In order to bound the above, we first note that:

\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tau^{\prime\prime})d\tau^{\prime\prime}=\frac{1}{10}\cdot 2^{-2j}\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime}}\frac{\mathbf{1}_{j,\tau^{\prime\prime}}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}d\tau^{\prime\prime}\leq\min\big(X^{-2},2^{-2j}\tau^{-2}\big)\leq\min\big(1,2^{-2j}\tau^{-2}\big).

In this bound we used the good control of the constant in the definition of
b(k),
obtained using the smallness of
\delta
in (
4.7
). As a result, we obtain for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

\prod_{j=l+1}^{k-1}\bigg(1+\int_{\tau}^{\tau^{\prime}}b(j)c(j,\tau^{\prime\prime})d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)\leq\prod_{j=l+1}^{k-1}\Big(\min\big(2,1+2^{-2j}\tau^{-2}\big)\Big)\lesssim 1+2^{-l}\tau^{-1},

where we bounded the terms with
2^{-j}<\tau
using the inequality
x+1\leq e^{x}.

Finally, we conclude by noticing that:

2^{-8k}2^{6l}\cdot 2^{-l}\tau^{-1}\leq 2^{-7k}2^{5l}\cdot\frac{2^{-k}}{\tau}\leq 2^{-7k}2^{5l}\cdot\frac{2}{X}\leq 2^{-7k}2^{5l}.

∎

We use the definition of
A_{k}(\tau)
to compute the new error terms obtained on the RHS of (
4.11
). For the data terms we compute:

d_{k}+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}d_{l}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim_{\delta}d_{k}+\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{5(l-k)}d_{l}\lesssim_{\delta}d_{k}.

In this inequality, we used the fact that
\sum_{k}\sum_{l}2^{-5|k-l|}d_{l}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II},
so the term
\sum_{l}2^{-5|k-l|}d_{l}
can be written schematically as
d_{k}.
We will use similar bounds implicitly for the rest of the proof.

Additionally, we have the following bound for the discrete error terms
S_{k}:

S_{k}(\tau)+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}S_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim S_{k}(\tau)+\delta X2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}\frac{2^{-3l+6m}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{6}}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}

\lesssim S_{k}(\tau)+\delta X2^{-7k}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau<X2^{-m-1}\\
x\leq m\leq l\end{subarray}}\int_{X2^{-l-1}}^{X2^{-m-1}}\frac{2^{-3l+6m}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{6}}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1})d\tau^{\prime}

\lesssim S_{k}(\tau)+\frac{\delta}{X^{4}}2^{-7k}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau<X2^{-m-1}\\
x\leq m\leq l\end{subarray}}2^{2l+6m}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\lesssim\frac{\delta}{X^{2}}2^{-5k}\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{6m}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1}).

Discrete Gronwall inequality.

We deal with the sum of discrete error terms obtained in (
4.11
) using the discrete Gronwall inequality. We first write the estimate in a convenient form by defining:

\displaystyle\widetilde{S}_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle:=\frac{\delta}{X^{2}}2^{-5k}\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{6m}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1}),

\displaystyle\widetilde{E}_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle:=E_{k}(\tau)+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}E_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime},

\displaystyle\widetilde{I}_{k}(\tau)

\displaystyle:=I_{k}(\tau)+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}I_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}.

Thus, (
4.11
) implies that for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\lesssim C_{\delta}d_{k}+\widetilde{S}_{k}(\tau)+C_{\delta}\widetilde{E}_{k}(\tau)+C_{\delta}\widetilde{I}_{k}(\tau).

(4.12)

In particular, there exist constants
C^{\prime\prime\prime},C_{\delta}>0
such that for any
k\geq x:

2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\leq C_{\delta}d_{k}+C_{\delta}\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+C_{\delta}\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+C^{\prime\prime\prime}\frac{\delta}{X^{2}}2^{-5k}\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}2^{6m}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1}).

We fix the parameters
X,\delta>0
satisfying (
4.6
), (
4.7
), and :

C^{\prime\prime\prime}\frac{\delta}{X^{2}}<\frac{1}{10}.

(4.13)

We also introduce the notation:

b_{k}=C_{\delta}d_{k}+C_{\delta}\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+C_{\delta}\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1}).

Using this notation, we have for any
k\geq x:

2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\leq b_{k}+\frac{1}{10}2^{-5k}\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}2^{5m}\cdot 2^{m}a_{m}(X2^{-m-1}).

We apply the discrete Gronwall inequality according to
[
Jon64
]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\leq b_{k}+\frac{1}{10}2^{-5k}\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}\bigg(2^{5m}b_{m}\cdot\prod_{j=m+1}^{k-1}\big(1+1/10\cdot 2^{-5j}\cdot 2^{5j}\big)\bigg)\lesssim b_{k}+2^{-4k}\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}2^{4m}b_{m}.

As a consequence, we have the following bound for all
k\geq x
:

2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\lesssim_{\delta}d_{k}+\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}2^{-4k+4m}\Big(\widetilde{E}_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+\widetilde{I}_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big).

(4.14)

Notation.
We used the parameters
X>0
and
\delta>0
to apply Gronwall-like inequalities in the above proof. We now fixed these parameters (depending on
M,C_{0},C_{2}
), so we return to our usual notation convention that we write
A\lesssim B
if there exists a constant
C>0
depending only on the constants
M,C_{0},C_{2}
such that
A\leq CB.

Consequences of the discrete Gronwall inequality.

We use the estimate (
4.14
) in the high frequency regime estimate (
4.12
). From (
4.14
) we obtain for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

\widetilde{S}_{k}(\tau)\lesssim\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-5k+5m}d_{m}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4k+4m}\Big(\widetilde{E}_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+\widetilde{I}_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)

\lesssim\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-5k+5m}d_{m}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4k+4m}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big),

where the second bound follows by:

\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4k+4m}\cdot 2^{-7m}\sum_{l=x}^{m-1}\int_{X2^{-l-1}}^{1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}E_{l}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\sum_{\tau<X2^{-l-1}\leq 1}2^{-4k+4l}E_{l}(X2^{-l-1}),

and we have a similar inequality for the inhomogeneous terms
I_{l}.
We also bound the remaining terms of (
4.12
):

\widetilde{E}_{k}(\tau)=E_{k}(\tau)+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}E_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim E_{k}(\tau)+2^{-2k}\tau^{-2}\sum_{l\geq x}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{l}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}

\lesssim E_{k}(\tau)+2^{-2k}\tau^{-2}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime},

\widetilde{I}_{k}(\tau)=I_{k}(\tau)+2^{-7k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\frac{2^{5l}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}I_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim I_{k}(\tau)+2^{-2k}\tau^{-2}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We combine these estimates and we obtain that (
4.12
) implies for all
\tau\in[X2^{-k-1},1]
:

2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{k,\tau}\lesssim d_{k}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-5k+5m}d_{m}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4k+4m}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)

+E_{k}(\tau)+I_{k}(\tau)+2^{-2k}\tau^{-2}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}+2^{-2k}\tau^{-2}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Summing the high frequency estimates.

For each
\tau\in(0,1],
we sum the above high frequency regime estimates for all
k\geq x
such that
X2^{-k-1}\leq\tau:

\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}\Big(E_{k}(\tau)+I_{k}(\tau)\Big)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)+

+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Additionally, we have the estimates:

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}E_{k}(\tau)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\geq x}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{k}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime},

\displaystyle\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}I_{k}(\tau)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Therefore, we obtain the following high frequency regime estimate:

\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.15)

Proof of Proposition
4.6
.

The final step in the proof of the optimal high frequency regime estimate (
4.5
) consists of bounding the second error term on the RHS of (
4.15
).

We first notice that we have the estimate for all
\tau\in(0,1]
:

\tau^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)\lesssim\tau^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\sum_{k\geq x}\tau^{3}2^{k}\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\lesssim\tau^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}\tau^{3}2^{k}\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\tau^{2}\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)

\lesssim\tau^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}\tau^{2}\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\tau^{2}\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)

\lesssim\tau^{2}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\tau^{2}\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau).

We use the preliminary estimates in Proposition
4.2
and (
4.15
) to get:

\tau^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using Gronwall, we proved that for all
\tau\in(0,1]
:

\tau^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.16)

Finally, we use this estimate in (
4.15
) to obtain the optimal high frequency estimate (
4.5
).

4.6
The main estimate in Theorem
1.2

In this section, we prove the main estimate (
1.13
) in Theorem
1.2
. For this, we first establish the following top order estimate for the singular component
\Phi_{0}:

\mathcal{E}^{0}_{II}(\tau)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\sum_{m=0}^{M}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime},

(4.17)

where we define the top order energy for the singular component:

\mathcal{E}^{0}_{II}(\tau):=\tau\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{M+1/2}}^{2}+\tau^{2}\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{M+3/2}}^{2}+\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\sum_{m=0}^{M-1}\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{m}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using Propositions
4.2
,
4.4
and the estimate (
4.5
), we have the bound for all
\tau\in(0,1]
:

\tau^{2}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)\lesssim\tau^{2}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\sum_{k\geq x}\tau^{2}2^{k}\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\lesssim\tau^{2}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}\tau^{2}2^{k}\Big(\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)+\tau\sum_{\tau\geq X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}a_{k}(\tau)

\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1}).

We use the bound (
4.14
) for
2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})
to get:

\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})

\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}E_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1}I_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We note that we used (
4.16
) in the last inequality. As a result, we proved that:

\tau^{2}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

As usual, we also get for all
\tau\in(0,1]
:

\displaystyle\tau\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}\cdot\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\sqrt{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3/2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\sqrt{\tau^{\prime}}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Applying Gronwall, we proved that:

\tau^{2}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\tau^{2}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+\tau\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We recall that
\xi=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}.
Combining the above estimate with the preliminary estimates in Proposition
4.2
, we proved (
4.17
).

Proof of (
1.13
).

We use the estimates for the regular quantities in Section
4.1
and the top order estimate for the singular quantities (
4.17
) to get:

\mathcal{E}_{II}(\tau)\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)+\sum_{m=0}^{M}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

We also recall our notation
F_{m}^{\prime}=\psi\sum_{i=1}^{I}\nabla^{m+1}\Phi_{i}+F_{m}^{0}.
Using again the estimates for the regular quantities proved in Section
4.1
, we conclude.
∎

4.7
Estimates for the asymptotic quantities

In this section we prove the estimates (
1.14
) and (
1.15
) for the asymptotic quantities
\mathcal{O}
,
\mathfrak{h},
and
\Phi_{i}^{0}
with
1\leq i\leq I,
in order to complete the proof of Theorem
1.2
. We notice that the estimates for the regular quantities in Proposition
4.1
already imply the bound (
1.26
) for
\Phi_{i}^{0}
with
1\leq i\leq I.
As a result, we only need to prove the estimates (
1.28
) and (
1.15
) for
\mathcal{O}
and
\mathfrak{h}
.

Proposition 4.7
.

The obstruction tensor
\mathcal{O}
satisfies the estimate (
1.28
):

\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0).

Proof.

As a consequence of the preliminary estimates in Proposition
4.2
, we obtain for all
0\leq m\leq M
:

\big\|\nabla^{m}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\bigg(\big\|\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{m+3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{H^{m+1/2}}^{2}\bigg)\bigg|_{\tau=1}+\int_{0}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{m}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0),

so it remains to prove the above bound for
\big\|\nabla^{M+1}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}
.

For any
k\geq x
and
0\leq\tau<X2^{-k-1}\leq 1
, the low frequency regime estimate in Proposition
4.4
implies:

2^{2k}\big\|\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using the expansion of
\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}
at
\mathcal{I}^{-}
, we get that for all
k\geq x:

2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{k}\int_{0}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+2^{-k}\int_{0}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Together with our previous estimate for
\|\mathcal{O}\|_{H^{M}},
we obtain that:

\big\|\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0)+\sum_{k\geq x}2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1}).

We complete the proof since we already proved in Section
4.6
:

\sum_{k\geq x}2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{0}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0).

∎

In order to prove the estimate (
1.15
) for
\mathfrak{h}
, we first prove that it can be reduced to the proof of (
1.29
). We recall the notation
\mathfrak{h}_{M}=\nabla^{M}h-2(\log\nabla)\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O},
and notice that we have the bounds:

\displaystyle\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\big[(\log\nabla),\nabla^{M}\big]\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\Big(\big\|\not{Riem}_{0}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}\Big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}+C\Big(\big\|\not{Riem}_{0}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}\Big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2},

where we used the proof of Lemma
3.2
to bound the commutator term. Using the interpolation inequalities of
[
KR06
]
and the bound in Lemma
2.7
, we obtain:

\big\|\mathfrak{h}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}\lesssim\big\|h\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+C\Big(\big\|\not{Riem}_{0}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}\Big)\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

Using the already established bound for
\mathcal{O},
we showed that it suffices to prove (
1.29
):

\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0).

We recall our notation
\xi=\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}
and we consider the renormalized quantities for all
k\geq x
:

\overline{\xi}_{k}=P_{k}\xi-\tau\log(2^{k}\tau)P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi.

Using the expansion for
\xi
at
\mathcal{I}^{-},
we get:

\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\overline{\xi}_{k}=\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\Big(P_{k}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}-\tau\log(2^{k}\tau)P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\nabla^{M}\Phi_{0}\Big)=P_{k}\nabla^{M}h-2\log(2^{k})P_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}.

Recalling the definition of the operator
R_{k}
in (
2.29
), we can write:

P_{k}\nabla^{M}h-2\log(2^{k})P_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}=P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}+2P_{k}(\log\nabla)\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}-2\log(2^{k})P_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}=P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}+R_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}.

Thus, we obtain using (
2.32
):

2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{2k}\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+2^{2k}\big\|R_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{2k}\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|\underline{P}_{k}\nabla^{M}\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}.

Summing for all
k\geq x
, we get that:

\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\mathfrak{h}_{M}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\big\|\mathcal{O}\big\|_{H^{M+1}}^{2}+\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

According to the above estimate and (
1.28
), in order to prove (
1.29
) we need to establish the following result:

Proposition 4.8
.

We have the following estimate:

\sum_{k\geq x}2^{2k}\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0).

As a result, (
1.15
) holds, completing the proof of Theorem
1.2
.

Proof.

Using (
4.4
) as in the proof of Proposition
4.4
, we get that
\overline{\xi}_{k}
satisfies the equation on
\tau\in(0,X2^{-k-1}]
:

\nabla_{\tau}\overline{\xi}_{k}=[\nabla_{\tau},P_{k}]\xi-\log(2^{k}\tau)\nabla_{\tau}\big(\tau P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big)=

=\tau[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\xi-\log(2^{k}\tau)\Big(4\tau\Delta P_{k}\xi+\tau P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\xi\big)+\tau[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\tau\nabla_{\tau}\xi+\tau P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\Big).

We contract by
\overline{\xi}_{k}
to obtain the energy estimate:

2^{2k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim\Big(2^{2k}\big\|P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\big\|P_{k}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)\Big|_{\tau={X2^{-k-1}}}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot 2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\big\|[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot|2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|\cdot\big\|\Delta P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot|2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|\cdot\big\|P_{k}\big(\psi\nabla\xi\big)\big\|_{L^{2}}+

+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot|2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|\cdot\big\|[\nabla_{4},P_{k}]\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}\cdot|2^{k}\tau^{\prime}\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|\cdot\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}.

We use Lemma
2.3
, Gronwall, and the notation in Section
4.5
to obtain:

\displaystyle 2^{2k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim

\displaystyle 2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\Delta P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{-k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{1}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\nabla P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\tau^{\prime}\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}.

We use the preliminary estimates in Proposition
4.2
to get:

2^{2k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim 2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+

+\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\Delta P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{k}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

Using the finite band property of the LP projections for
P_{k}=\underline{P}_{k}^{2}
, we have the bound:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\Delta P_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{3k}(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})^{2}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\big\|\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

(4.18)

Once we established the above estimate for
2^{k}\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\|_{L^{2}},
the idea of the proof is to decompose the error term in (
4.18
) into its low frequency and high frequency regime parts, in order to use our previous estimates. For each
\tau^{\prime}\in[\tau,X2^{-k-1}]
we have:

\big\|\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\sum_{l<x}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}+\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}.

(4.19)

The first three terms in (
4.19
) are in the low frequency regime, and the last term is in the high frequency regime.

We bound the
first term
in (
4.19
) using the
L^{2}
almost orthogonality of the LP projections:

\sum_{l<x}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\sum_{l<x}2^{-5k+5l}\big\|P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim 2^{-5k}\big\|\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime}).

The corresponding term in (
4.18
) is bounded using Proposition
4.2
:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{-6k}(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\cdot\tau^{\prime}\big\|\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim 2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-k}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau).

For the
second term
in (
4.19
) we use Cauchy-Schwarz and we consider a projection operator with
\underline{P}_{k}=\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{2}
:

\displaystyle\bigg(\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{2}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime})\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-5(k-l)}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-2k}2^{-5(k-l)}\big\|\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\tau^{\prime})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-2k}2^{-5(k-l)}\big(2^{-2l}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})+2^{-3l}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-3l}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau^{\prime})\big),

where we used Proposition
4.4
in the second line. The corresponding term in (
4.18
) is bounded by:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{k}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}2^{-3(k-l)}\bigg(2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})+2^{-l}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-l}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau^{\prime})\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim 2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-k}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)+\sum_{l=x}^{k-1}2^{-3(k-l)}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1}).

For the
third term
in (
4.19
), we have similarly from Proposition
4.4
:

\bigg(\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}^{2}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime})\bigg)^{2}\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}2^{-5(l-k)}\big\|\underline{\widetilde{P}}_{k}P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}2^{-2k}2^{-5(l-k)}\big\|\nabla P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\tau^{\prime})

\lesssim\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{2}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}2^{-2k}2^{-5(l-k)}\big(2^{-2l}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})+2^{-3l}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-3l}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau^{\prime})\big).

As a result, the corresponding term in (
4.18
) is bounded using Proposition
4.4
:

\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-l-1}}2^{k}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}2^{-7(l-k)}\big(2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})+2^{-l}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-l}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau^{\prime})\big)d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}l\geq k\\
\tau<X2^{-l-1}\end{subarray}}\big(2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})+2^{-l}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-l}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)\big)\cdot\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-l-1}}2^{k}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}2^{-7(l-k)}d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim 2^{-k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{-k}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)+\sum_{l\geq k}2^{-7(l-k)}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1}).

For the high frequency regime
fourth term
in (
4.19
), we have using our notation in Section
4.5
:

\bigg(\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}\big\|P_{l}^{2}\underline{P}_{k}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}(\tau^{\prime})\bigg)^{2}\lesssim\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-5(l-k)}\big\|P_{l}\xi\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}(\tau^{\prime})\lesssim\tau^{\prime}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-5(l-k)}a_{l}(\tau^{\prime}).

The corresponding term in (
4.18
) is bounded in Lemma
4.2
at the end of the section.

We combine the previous bounds and we get from (
4.18
) that for all
\tau\in(0,X2^{-k-1}]
:

2^{2k}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\lesssim d_{k}+\tau^{6}2^{6k}\mathcal{D}_{II}+2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\sum_{l\geq x}2^{-3|k-l|}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(X2^{-l-1})

+\big(2^{-k}+\tau^{6}2^{6k}\big)\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)+\sum_{l\geq k}2^{-6(l-k)}\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{l}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{l}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}+\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+

+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4|k-m|}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)+\sum_{l\geq k}2^{-6(l-k)}\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{l}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}.

We take the limit
\tau\rightarrow 0
and sum for all
k\geq x:

\displaystyle\sum_{k\geq x}\Big(2^{2k}\lim_{\tau\rightarrow 0}\big\|\overline{\xi}_{k}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(0)+\sum_{k\geq x}2^{k}a_{k}(X2^{-k-1})

\displaystyle+\sum_{k\geq x}\big(\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})\big)+\int_{0}^{1}(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\Big(\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}.

For the last three terms, we use the estimates in Section
4.5
and we obtain the conclusion.
∎

We conclude the section by proving the additional estimate used in the previous proof:

Lemma 4.2
.

We have the bound for all
\tau\in(0,X2^{-k-1}]
:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})^{3}|\log(2^{k}\tau^{\prime})|^{2}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}\cdot 2^{l}a_{l}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim d_{k}+\tau^{6}2^{6k}\cdot\big(\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)\big)+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4|k-m|}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)+

+\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\sum_{l\geq k}2^{-6(l-k)}\bigg(\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{l}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{l}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{l}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\bigg)+2^{-k}\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau).

Proof.

We use the estimate (
4.12
) as a starting point. We also use the bound on
\widetilde{S}_{l}
in Section
4.5
, and the definitions of
\widetilde{E}_{l}
and
\widetilde{I}_{l}
. Thus, we have for all
\tau\in[X2^{-l-1},1]
:

2^{l}a_{l}(\tau)\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau}\lesssim d_{l}+\widetilde{S}_{l}(\tau)+\widetilde{E}_{l}(\tau)+\widetilde{I}_{l}(\tau)

\lesssim d_{l}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-5l+5m}d_{m}+\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4l+4m}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)+

+E_{l}(\tau)+I_{l}(\tau)+2^{-7l}\int_{\tau}^{1}\sum_{m=x}^{l-1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime})\Big)\mathbf{1}_{m,\tau^{\prime}}d\tau^{\prime}.

We use this bound to obtain a total of 8 error terms that control our main integral. We notice that the first two terms can be dealt with in a straightforward way. For the third and fourth terms we compute that:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\sum_{\tau^{\prime}<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}2^{-4l+4m}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1}2^{-4|k-m|}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big),

where we used the fact that
l\geq m
and
l\geq k
in the first line. For the fifth and sixth terms we have:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}\Big(E_{l}(\tau^{\prime})+I_{l}(\tau^{\prime})\Big)d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim

\lesssim\sum_{l\geq k}2^{-6(l-k)}\bigg(\int_{\tau}^{1}e_{l}(\tau^{\prime})d\tau^{\prime}+\int_{\tau}^{1}2^{l}\tau^{\prime}\big\|P_{l}F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{L^{2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\bigg)+2^{-k}\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}.

For the seventh and eight terms we can write:

\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}\cdot\bigg(2^{-7l}\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\sum_{m=x}^{l-1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Big)\mathbf{1}_{m,\tau^{\prime\prime}}d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}\lesssim I+II+III,

where we introduce the notation:

\displaystyle I

\displaystyle=\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}\cdot 2^{-7l}\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}\bigg(\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Big)\mathbf{1}_{m,\tau^{\prime\prime}}d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime},

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle=\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}\cdot 2^{-7l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1\\
k\leq m<l\end{subarray}}\bigg(\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Big)\mathbf{1}_{m,\tau^{\prime\prime}}d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime},

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle=\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{-6(l-k)}\cdot 2^{-7l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau>X2^{-m-1}\\
m<l\end{subarray}}\bigg(\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Big)d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}.

We first notice that in the inner integral of
I
we have
\tau^{\prime\prime}\geq X2^{-m-1}>X2^{-k-1}.
Thus, we have the bound:

I\lesssim 2^{-7k}\sum_{m=x}^{k-1}\bigg(\int_{X2^{-k-1}}^{1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Big)\mathbf{1}_{m,\tau^{\prime\prime}}d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)\lesssim\widetilde{E}_{k}(X2^{-k-1})+\widetilde{I}_{k}(X2^{-k-1}),

where in the second inequality we used the definitions of
\widetilde{E}_{k}
and
\widetilde{I}_{k}.
Next, we have the bound:

\displaystyle II

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{6k-13l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1\\
k\leq m<l\end{subarray}}\bigg(\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)\int_{\tau^{\prime}}^{1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\mathbf{1}_{m,\tau^{\prime\prime}}d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{l\geq k}\mathbf{1}_{l,\tau^{\prime}}2^{6k-13l}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq 1\\
k\leq m<l\end{subarray}}2^{7m}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq X2^{-k-1}}2^{7m}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big)\cdot\sum_{l>m}2^{6k-13l}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\tau<X2^{-m-1}\leq X2^{-k-1}}2^{-6|m-k|}\Big(E_{m}(X2^{-m-1})+I_{m}(X2^{-m-1})\Big),

where we used again
\tau^{\prime\prime}\geq X2^{-m-1}
to compute the inner integral in the first line.

Finally, we notice that the last error term
III
must vanish as
\tau\rightarrow 0,
since the inner sum would be empty. We prove a bound consistent with this expectation:

\displaystyle III

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{\tau>X2^{-m-1}}\bigg(\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{2^{5m}}{(\tau^{\prime\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime\prime})\Big)d\tau^{\prime\prime}\bigg)\cdot\int_{\tau}^{X2^{-k-1}}2^{k}\sum_{m<l}2^{-6(l-k)}\cdot 2^{-7l}d\tau^{\prime}

\displaystyle\lesssim 2^{6k}\sum_{\tau>X2^{-m-1}}2^{-8m}\bigg(\int_{\tau}^{1}\frac{1}{(\tau^{\prime})^{3}}\Big(E_{m}(\tau^{\prime})+I_{m}(\tau^{\prime})\Big)d\tau^{\prime}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\tau}^{1}\Big(\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}+(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\xi\big\|_{H^{3/2}}^{2}+(\tau^{\prime})^{3}\big\|\nabla_{\tau}\xi\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}\Big)d\tau^{\prime}\cdot\tau^{-2}2^{6k}\sum_{\tau>X2^{-m-1}}2^{-8m}

\displaystyle\lesssim\tau^{6}2^{6k}\bigg(\mathcal{D}_{II}+\int_{\tau}^{1}\tau^{\prime}\big\|F_{M}^{\prime}\big\|_{H^{1/2}}^{2}d\tau^{\prime}\bigg)\lesssim\tau^{6}2^{6k}\big(\mathcal{D}_{II}+\mathcal{F}_{II}(\tau)\big).

Collecting all the bounds proved for the error terms, we conclude the proof.
∎
